# Corporate and private equity brand directory

US-focused storefront and fuel-brand directory, reviewed against primary company, investor, and regulatory sources. Coverage is not exhaustive; related international brands may appear for reference.

1195 distinct brands, with overlapping categories: 914 corporate and 279 private equity backed. Review dates are recorded per entry. This is a reviewed directory, not a complete list of every brand or an automatic ownership feed.

Filters describe brand affiliation. They do not verify the owner of an individual franchise, licensed store, or fuel station. Corporate includes every verified stock-exchange-listed company and its owned brands, regardless of size, plus large private chains. Publicly traded companies with PE backing appear in both sections and match either exclusion filter. A listed financial sponsor alone does not make its private portfolio companies public. Regional records use geographic matching guards when unrelated chains share a name; the coordinates do not verify an individual store’s owner. PE includes documented minority investments, explained per entry. Ownership follows where most of the money goes. Franchise and dealer brands stay labeled by their corporate owner because royalties, fees, and supply purchases flow mainly to the brand, even when a local owner runs the store. Cooperative and licensed-independent networks, such as Ace Hardware and IGA, are labeled independent / co-op because most of the money stays with the local owner. Unknown businesses are never assumed independent. Former-chain records, including Rite Aid and Bartell Drugs, identify legacy brand affiliation; their notes describe closures and do not establish that a location is still open.

Edit `data/brands.json`, then run `npm run directory`, `npm test`, and `npm run build`. The app matcher, browser list, JSON, CSV, and this document are generated from the same records.
